\[Radha says\] “O Krsna! The red of your lips becomes similar in color to your body. Your red lips have become black. They have become black from kissing eyes stained with black mascara. This clearly shows your devious nature.”

It is said that kisses are placed on the cheek, eyes and forehead. That his lips are stained with mascara indicates his lack of expertise. *Dasana-vasas* means lips according to *Amara-kosa.*

### Text 8.4

*vapur anuharati tava smara-sangara-khara-nakhara-ksata-rekham \|*  
*marakata-sakala-kalita-kala-dhauta-liper iva rati-jaya-lekham \|\|4\|\|*

*hari hari...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vapur* - Body; *anuharati* - resembles; *tava* - your; *smara* - of love; *sangara* - in the battle; *khara* - sharp; *nakhara* - with fingernails; *ksata* - \[are\] inflicted; *rekham* - whose scratches; *marakata* - of emerald; *sakala* - on a piece; *kalita* - made; *kala*-*dhauta* - of gold; *liper -* of letters; *iva* - as if; *rati* - of Cupid; *jaya* - of victory; *lekham* - a letter.

**Перевод:**

**“Your body resembles a victory letter of Cupid, with fingernail injuries inflicted during the battle of love, appearing like golden letters inscribed on a sapphire gem.**

**“Ah! Go away Madhava...**

**Комментарий:**

Your body resembles (*anuharati*) a victory letter of Cupid (*rati-jaya-lekham*), in which Cupid says “I have conquered this body.” The body has lines produced by injuries from sharp fingernails, arising in the battle of love, which resemble golden (*kala-dhauta*) words written on a piece of sapphire. Krsna’s black body is compared to a piece of sapphire. The marks from the nails are compared to a golden inscription. Gold is often described as reddish by poets. *Kaladauta* means gold according to *Visva.* *Sakala* means a piece. *Bokhara* means fingernail

### Text 8.5

*carana-kamala-galad-alaktaka-siktam idam tava hrdayam udaram \|*  
*darsayatlva bahir madana-druma-nava-kisalaya-parivaram \|\|5\|\|*

*hari hari...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*carana-kamala* - From a \[woman’s\] lotus-foot; *galad* - flowing; *alaktaka* - by lac; *siktam* - watered; *idam* - this; *tava* - your; *hrdayam* - heart; *udaram* - attractive; *darsayati* - shows; *iva* - as if; *bahir* - externally; *madana* - of love (or of Cupid); *druma* - \[arising\] from the tree; *nava* - new; *kisalaya* - of shoots; *parivaram* - the host.

**Перевод:**

**“Your attractive heart externally shows the host of new shoots arising from the tree of love, watered by lac flowing from a woman’s lotus foot on your chest.**

**“Ah! Go away Madhava...**

**Комментарий:**

\[Krsna says\] “Forgetting the path, as I was coming, I was wounded by thorns on my body. That is what you see, not nail marks of a woman.”

\[Radha says\] “Your heart here externally shows a group of new sprouts growing from the tree of love within your heart.” Or *nava* can mean praiseworthy since shoots are new by their nature. *Nava* means praise according to *Visva-kosa*. The sprouts are watered by lac flowing from a woman’s lotus foot. Other trees however show sprouts after being watered at the root. This is the ornament called *rupaka* (metaphor). Your heart is very attractive (*udaram*) or huge, incapable of being hidden. The lotus feet indicate good fragrance. The tree of Cupid indicates attraction. Watered with lac indicates the coolness of the tree. The amorous position is called *kraunca.* *Parivaram* means all.

### Text 8.6

*dasana-padam bhavad-adhara-gatam mama janayati cetasi khedam*  
*kathayati katham adhunapi maya saha tava vapur etad abhedam \|\|6\|\|*

*hari hari...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*dasana* - Of teeth; *padam* - the mark; *bhavad* - your; *adhara* - on lips; *gatam* - situated; *mama* - my; *janayati* - produces; *cetasi* - in heart; *khedam* - pain, *kathayati katham* - Why do you say; *adhunapi* - even now; *maya* - me; *saha* - with; *tava* - your; *vapur* - body; *etad* - this; *abhedam* - \[is\] identical?

**Перевод:**

**“Why do you now say that your body is one with mine? The tooth marks on your lips give pain to my heart.**

**“Ah! Go away Madhava...**

**Комментарий:**

\[Krsna says\] “Sympathizing with your lamentation, I feel sickness in my heart. How can you think otherwise?”

\[Radha thinks\] “That is true. If you were not one with me, I should not have pain from seeing the wounds on your body.” Two bodies are not different if the injuries in one body are experienced as pain in another body. With this in mind she speaks.

The marks of teeth on your lips give me pain. How can you now say your body is one with me? Indirectly it is inferred “How can our bodies be one since I experience great suffering on seeing the signs of enjoyment (not suffering) of another woman on your body?”

By thinking of causes of difference, non-difference (oneness) is denied. This is an example of *vibhavana alahkara.* *Dandi* describes this:

*prasiddha-hetu-vyavrttya yat kincit karanantaram \|*  
*yatra svabhavikatvam va bhavyam sa ca vibhavana \|\|*

***Vibhavana*** **refers to thinking of another cause or assuming a natural condition, while excluding the well-known cause.**

### Text 8.7

*bahir iva malinatararn tava krsna mano’pi bhavisyati пuпат \|*  
*katham atha vancayase janam anugatam asama-sara-jvara-dunam \|\|7\|\|*

*hari hari...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bahir* - Externally; *iva* - as if; *malinataram* - contamination; *tava* - your; *krsna* - о Krsna; *mano* - mind; *api* - even; *bhavisyati* - will become; *nunam* - for certain, *katham* - Why; *atha* - then; *vancayase* - do you cheat; *janam* - people; *anugatam* - surrendered \[to you\]; *asama* - of Cupid; *sara -* of the arrows; *jvara* - from the pain; *dunam* - \[and\] suffering?

**Перевод:**

**“For certain, your mind will show its contamination externally. Why do you cheat persons who are surrendered to you and are suffering from the pains of Cupid?**

**“Ah! Go away Madhava...**

**Комментарий:**

\[Krsna says\] “I broke off a lotus to bring to you, but a bee suddenly emerged from the flower and bit my lip. This is not an injury from a woman’s tooth.”

\[Radha says\] “O Krsna! It is certain that your mind will become very contaminated like your body.”

“How can you know about contamination in my mind?” Atha indicates a question.

“Why do you cheat a person like me, dedicated to you, suffering from Cupid?”. This means it is not just internal contamination. The internal contamination of cheating a surrendered person cannot remain internal. The great contamination is visible from his trying to hide his injuries arising from teeth.

*Atha* indicates a question according to Visva. *Utpreksa* ornament is used. *Dandi* says:

*manye sanke dhruvam prayo nimam ity evam adibhih \|*  
*utpreksa vyajyate sabdair iva-sabdo’pi tadrsah \|\|*

***Utpreksa*** **(simile) is indicated by use of words like** ***nunam manye, sanke,dhruvan*** **and** ***prayah*** **which indicate similarity, like** ***iva*****.**

### Text 8.8

*bhramati bhavan abala-kavalaya vanesu kim atra vicitram \|*  
*prathayati putanikaiva vadhu-vadha-nirdaya-bala-caritram \|\|8\|\|*

*hari hari...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bhramati* - Wanders; *bhavan* - your honor; *abala* - feeble women; *kavalaya* - for devouring; *vanesu* - in forests, *kim* - What; *atra* - with respect to that; *vicitram* - \[is\] astonishing? *prathayati* - Reveals; *putanikaiva* - indeed Putanika; *vadhu* - of young women; *vadha* - in the killing; *nirdaya* - merciless; *bala* - baby; *caritram* - \[your\] behavior.

**Перевод:**

**“You wander in the forests in order to kill innocent women. That is not astonishing. The case of Putana has shown that even as a baby you were merciless in killing women.**

**“Ah! Go away Madhava...”**

**Комментарий:**

She clarifies what she has said by showing the cruel effects.

“You wander in the forests in order to devour young women. What is astonishing about that? Nothing.”

The reason for his devouring of women is given. The killing of Putana, who was sent by Kamsa, shows your merciless nature as a child in killing women. As a baby you killed a Raksasi who was giving her breast to you. In your *kaisora* age now, is it astonishing if you kill girls with tender limbs like me?

Or, О merciless killer of women! Putana proclaims your infant act (*bala-caritam*) of killing the sister of Kamsa. The suffix *ka* in *putanika* has no significance. The *khandita* (betrayed) heroine is described in this verse. *Bharata* says:

*nidra-kasaya-kalusikrta-tamra-netro*  
*nari-nakha-vrana-vicitritangah \|*  
*yasya kuto’pi grham eti patih prabhate*  
*sa khanditeti kathita havibhih puranaih \|\|*

**The betrayed woman is described by ancient poets as follows. Her husband comes from somewhere to the house in the morning. His eyes are red because of the contamination of sleep and he has marks of women’s nails on various parts of his body.**

### Text 8.9

*sri-jayadeva-bhanita-rati-vancita-khandita-yuvati-vilapam \|*  
*srnuta sudha-madhuram vibudha vibudhalayatopi durapam \|\|9\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sri-jayadeva* - By Sri Jayadeva; *bhanita* - described; *rati* - of love; *vancita* - cheated; *khandit*a - \[and\] betrayed; *yuvati* - of the girl; *vilapam* - the lamentation; *srnuta* - hear; *sudha* - \[as\] nectar; *madhuram* - sweet; *vibudha* - о intelligent people; *vibudhalayato* - for the devatas; *api* - even; *durapam* - difficult to attain.

**Перевод:**

**О intelligent people! Please hear the lamentation of the girl betrayed, cheated of love, as described by Jayadeva. It is the sweetest nectar, not attained even by the devatas. (END OF SONG SEVENTEEN)COMMENTARY**

**Комментарий:**

О learned people who taste Krsna’s *madhurya-rasa*! Please hear the lamentation of the betrayed lover, devoid of enjoyment with Krsna as described by Jayadeva. It is sweeter than nectar. It cannot be attained even in Svarga. It is that rare. It is said:

*tasyaiva hetoh prayateta kovido*  
*na labhyate yad bhramatam upary adhah \|*  
*tal labhyate duhkhavad anyatah sukham*  
*kalena sarvatra gabhira-ramhasa \|\|*

**The wise man strives for that happiness which is not attained by Jivas wandering from Brahma-loka to the lowest species. Material happiness is attained in all cases without endeavor, by the law of** ***karma*****, just like distress, through the force of fast moving time. (SB 1.5.18)**

*tava kathamrtam tapta-jivanam*  
*kavibhir iditam kalmasapaham*  
*sravana-mangalam srimad atatam*  
*bhuvi grnanti ye bhuri-da janah*

**\[The** ***gopis*** **said,\] “The nectar of your topics is the life of those suffering in this material world. These narrations, praised by learned sages, eradicate one’s sinful reactions and bestow good fortune upon whoever hears them. These narrations are most excellent and are broadcast all over the world. Those who speak these topics are the greatest benefactors.” (SB 10.31.9)**

Or, it is harder to attain than Vaikuntha (*vibudhalayatah*) since Krsna and Radha are the *alambana* of unlimited *prema*. It is said in Bhagavatam:

*yad-vanchaya srir lalanacarat tapo*  
*vihaya kaman su-ciram dhrta-vrata*

**\[The wives of the Kaliya serpent said,\] “For this end, the goddess of fortune performed austerities for centuries, giving up all other desires and taking austere vows.” (SB 10.16.36)**

*Vibudha* means a learned person or a devata according to *Visva.*

There is another meaning. О hear about the false enactment of betrayal with attraction (*rati*), having a variety of symptoms in separation. Or hear about the dry crying of the young woman whose lips were bitten and was cheated in love, all on the pretext of obtaining enjoyment. Thus she cried out “Go away, go away.” That scene is rarely attained by the devatas, and tastier than nectar. \[Following is the other meaning of the song from 8.2 to 8.9\]

Krsna says, “I am grieving because of love. Do not say that I should go away. Shall I follow you to enjoy?”

Radha says, “That is not my desire. Go and follow her.”

“Your eye carries the attraction or color of Cupid. Why do you cheat me?” (verse 8.2)

“Your body is filled with intense lust, since it is black (the color of *srngara-rasa*). Because of that, you red lips have become black like your body. You actually see everything in terms of lust. Thus your lips were blackened by kissing the eyes of a woman anointed with mascara." (verse 8.3)

She then speaks verses 8.4 and 8.5.

Then Krsna speaks. “Let that be. Please give a festival of love to me who is your beloved.” He then speaks of oneness. “We are one. If you were not one with me, why could you say that your body is non-different?” (verse 8.6)

Radha says, “Cheater! Do you desire that again? How can you injure an obedient person with your nails and then cheat?” (verse 8.7). She continues till the end of verse 8.8.

In this way, the crest jewels of love (Radha and Krsna), completely overcome with various gestures and moods, enjoyed and spoke in a joking way. This is described by the famous poet in eight verses (verses 8.2 - 8.9). It will be said *adhara-sudha-pane katha-narmabhih*: with joking words he drank the nectar of her lips. This explanation also agrees with the next verse. It is spoken with jokes, out of enthusiasm for play. She feels embarrassed in front of the *sakhis* by the display of attraction to amorous play. She laments from pain because of her soft limbs during extreme enjoyment.

### Text 8.10

*tavedam pasyantyah prasarad-anuragam bahir iva*  
*priya-padalakta-cchuritam aruna-cchaya-hrdayam \|*  
*mamadya prakhyata-pranaya-bhara-bhangena kitava*  
*tvad-alokah sokad api kim api lajjam janayati \|\|10\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tava* - Your; idam - this; *pasyantyah* - for one who sees prasarad - \[is\] strong; *anurdgam* - whose love; *bahir* - externally; *iva* - as if; *priya* - of a lover; *pada* - from the foot; *alakta* - with the lac; *cchuritam* - \[is\] anointed; *aruna* - \[is\] red; *cchdya* - whose color; *hrdayam* - chest; *mama* - my; *adya* - today; *prakhyata* - famous; *pranaya* - of \[our\] attraction; *bhara* - great; *bhangena* - because of \[your\] breaking; *kitava* - о cheater; *tvad* - of you; *alokah* - the sight; *sokad* - beyond lamentation; *api* - even; *kim*  *api* - unspeakable; *lajjam* - shame; *janayati* - produces.

**Перевод:**

**\[Radha continues,\] “O cheater! Seeing you today produces shame more than lamentation because you broke our famous, intense relationship. I see your chest, stained red from the lac of a lover’s foot, as if your love for her has become externalized.”**

**Комментарий:**

О cheater! Seeing you produces unspeakable (*kim api*) shame more than lamentation, because of the destruction of our famous, intense attraction. Seeing you also produces lamentation, but more than lamentation it produces shame. In your presence as a lover, seeing you produces the greatest bliss. Now since you are not my lover, seeing you produces lamentation. I see your heart directly (*idam*) covered with lac from the foot of another lover. It is beautiful red.

Another version has *aruna-dyoti* (shining red) instead of *aruna-cchaya* (red color). Your chest shines like the color of sunrise. It is like attraction spilling out of your heart because it is so full. More unfortunate than lamentation is the worry of being mocked by women in the opposing camp. *Chayya* means beauty. *Aruna* means the color of sunrise or red.

### Text 8.11

*antar-mohana-mauli-ghurnana-calan-mandara-visramsana-*  
*stabdhakarsana-drsti-harsana-maha-mantrah kurangi-drsam \|*  
*drpyad-danava-duyamana-divisad-durvara-duhkhapadam*  
*bhramsah kamsa-ripor vyapohayatu vah sreyamsi vamsi-ravah \|\|11\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*antar* - heart; *mohana* - for \[their\] bewilderment; *mauli* - of \[their\] heads; *ghurnana* - for the shaking; *calan* - trembling; *mandara* - of \[their\] mandara flowers; *visramsana* - for the falling-down; *stabdha* - of the proud \[women\]; *akarsana* - for the bringing under control; *drsti* - to \[their\] eyes; *harsana* - \[and\] for the delight; *maha* - great; *mantrah* - a *mantra*; *kurangi*-*drsam* - of doe-eyed women; *drpyad* -proud; *danava* - by demons; *duyamana* - afflicted; *divisad* - of the devatas; *durvara* - unavoidable; *duhkha* - of the pain; *apadam* - of the obtaining; *bhramsah* - \[and\] the decrease; *kamsa-ripor* - of the destroyer of Kamsa (Krsna); *vyapohayatu* - may \[it\] bestow; *vah* - upon you; *sreyamsi* - auspiciousness; *vamsi* - of the flute; *ravah* - the sound.

**Перевод:**

**May the sound of Krsna’s (the destoryer of Kamsa flute, a powerful mantra that causes bewilderment in the hearts of doe-eyed women, makes them shake their heads in approval, makes the mandara flowers in their hair fall to the ground, brings even proud women under control, makes the eyes open wide in joy, and allays devatas’ worries of unavoidable pain inflicted by proud demons, bestow auspiciousness upon you!**

**Комментарий:**

The poet offers blessings at the end of the chapter.

May the sound of Krsna’s flute bestow (*vyapohay atu*) auspiciousness on you. Destroyer of Karhsa (*kamsa-ripoh*) indicates his ability to give auspiciousness. The sound is described as follows. It is a powerful mantra for producing results in the form of making eyes of doe-eyed women open on hearing it, bringing even proud women under control (*stabdha akarsana*), making the flowers on their heads fall to the ground (*visramsana*) when late in going to meet him, makes their heads nod in praise, and bewilders their minds. That sound destroys the devatas’ worries arising from unavoidable suffering inflicted by proud demons. Hearing the sound, the devatas are free of fear of suffering from demons. This description indicates the great ability of the hero. Moreover, the flute produces delight in the *devatas.* What then to speak of the delight they receive from the pastimes of the Supreme Lord?

## Chapter Nine. Mugdha-mukundah - Attractive Mukunda

### Text 9.1

*tam atha manmatha-khinnam*  
*rati-rasa-bhinnam visada-sampannam \|*  
*anucintita-hari-caritam*  
*kalahantaritam uvaca rahasi sakhi \|\|1\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tam* - To her (Radha); *atha* - then; *manmatha* - because of Cupid; *khinnam* - suffering; *rati* - of love; *rasa* - by the *srngara-rasa*; *bhinnam* - broken; *visada* - of distress; *sampannam* - possessed; *anucintita* - \[were\] repeatedly thought; *hari* - with Krsna; *caritam* - by whom pastimes; *kalaha* - because of \[her\] harsh words; *antaritam* - \[and\] having separated \[from Krsna\]; *uvaca* - spoke; *rahasi* - privately; *sakhi* - a female friend.

**Перевод:**

**The sakhi spoke privately to distressed Radha who was suffering because of love, whose heart had filled with srngara-rasa, who had rejected her lover with harsh words and who was then continually thinking of his previous pastimes.**

**Комментарий:**

A *sakhi* speaks to Radha who repents after castigating Krsna out of anger in love. The word *atha* indicates auspiciousness or “shortly after that.” The *sakhi* spoke in a private place to Radha, who was repenting after seperation from her lover, unto whom she spoke harsh words (*kalahantaritam*). She was suffering because of love. Her heart had been mixed with *srngara-rasa* or torn apart by attachment to enjoyment and therefore was distressed (*visada-sampannam*). She repeatedly remembered the previous pastimes she had with Krsna or his upright character at that time. This is the *kalahantarita* heroine. *Bharata* describes her:

*pranesvaram pranaya-kopa-bharena bhitam*  
*ya catu-karam acirad avadhirya pascat \|*  
*santapyate madana-vahni-sikha-sahasrair*  
*baspakula ca kalahantarita tu sa syat \|\|*

**The** ***kalahantarita*** **woman is full of tears and is pained by thousands of Cupid’s fiery arrows, after having disrespected with intense anger in love, her frightened lover who spoke pleasing words.**

*Bhinna* means mixed or split.

**\|\| Song Eighteen \|\|**

*gurjari-raga yati-tala*

**The song is sung in the** ***raga*** **of** ***gurjari*** **and in the** ***tala*** **of** ***yati.***

### Text 9.2

*harir abhisarati vahati madhu-pavane \|*  
*kim aparam adhika-sukham sakhi bhuvane \|\|2\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru manini manam aye \|\| dhru. \|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*harir* - Krsna; *abhisarati -* is coming to meet \[you\]; *vahati* - \[while it\] blows; *madhu -* of Spring; *pavane* - the breeze, *kim* - What; *aparam* - other; *adhika* - great; *sukham* - happiness; *sakhi* - о female friend; *bhuvane* - \[is there\] in the world?

*madhave* - With Krsna; *ma* - do not; *kuru* - create; *manini* - о angry woman; *manam* - anger; *aye* - o! (or having good fortune)

**Перевод:**

**\[The sakhi speaks to Radha\] “He is coming to meet you while the spring breeze blows. О friend, who can give greater happiness in this world?**

**“O angry woman! Do not be angry with Krsna. (chorus)**

**Комментарий:**

О angry woman! Do not be angry with Krsna. ‘*Aye*’ (having good fortune) can also modify *madhave* in the verse: Do not show anger, which will delay meeting him. The word Madhava indicates he will please Radha, though he is the husband of Laksmi. Therefore do not be angry. This is the chorus.

While the spring breeze blows, Krsna is coming. О friend! What great happiness is there in the three worlds, other than (*aparam*) meeting Krsna? There is no other happiness. Or, what happiness is experienced with Laksmi and others in Vaikuntha? None at all. *Hari* means he who can destroy your pain.

### Text 9.3

*tala-phalad api gurum atisarasam \|*  
*kim viphali-kuruse kuca-kalasam \|\|3\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tala* - Tala; *phalad -* than the fruit; *api* - even; *gurum -* \[which are\] better; *atisarasam* - \[and\] very full of *rasa*; *him* - why; *viphali* -useless; *kuruse* - do you render; *kuca-kalasam* - \[your\] pot-breasts?

**Перевод:**

**“Why do you make your pot-like breasts useless? They are better than the tala fruit and are full of srngara-rasa.**

**“O angry woman...**

**Комментарий:**

Why do you make your pot-like breasts useless? They will be successful by offering them to Krsna’s hands. Otherwise they are useless. They are better than tala fruits, since they are firmer and bigger. They are filled with *srngara-rasa*. Just as the *tala* fruit by squeezing produces juice, so your breasts by squeezing produce *srngara-rasa*. A pot is offered for auspiciousness to the lover’s hand when the lover comes. You should do the same with your breasts.

### Text 9.4

*kati na kathitam idam anupadam aciram \|*  
*ma parihara harim atisaya-ruciram \|\|4\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kati* - How many times; *na* - not; *kathitam* - \[was\] spoken \[by me\]; *idam* - this; *anupadam* - at every step; *aciram* - at this time? *ma* - Do not; *parihara* - give up; *harim* - Krsna; *atisaya* - preemminently; *ruciram* - attractive.

**Перевод:**

**“At this time did I not tell you many times about this at every step? Do not give up most attractive Krsna.**

**“O angry woman...**

**Комментарий:**

Did I say nothing about this at every step at this time (*aciram*)? Yes, I did. Do not disregard the very attractive Krsna who steals the heart.

### Text 9.5

*kim iti visidasi rodisi vikala \|*  
*vihasati yuvati-sabha tava sakala \|\|5\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*kim* - Why; *iti* - so; *visidasi* - are you emotional; *rodisi* - \[and\] weeping; *vikala* - depressed? *vihasati* - Are laughing \[at this\]; *yuvati* - of young women; *sabha* - party; *tava* - your; *sakala* - entire.

**Перевод:**

**“Why are you depressed? You are emotional and weeping. All the young women of your party are laughing at this.**

**“O angry woman...**

**Комментарий:**

\[Sakhi asks\] “Why do you lament?”

\[Radha replies\] “I am not lamenting.”

“You weep while being upset. All (*sakala*) the young women are laughing.” Or, “they laugh at your actions.” Or, “the young woman endowed with the sixty four arts, expert in *rasa* (*sakala*), laugh.”

### Text 9.6

*sajala-nalini-dala-silita-sayane \|*  
*harim avalokaya saphalaya nayane \|\|6\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sajala* - Moist; *nalini* - of the lotus; *dala* - of the petals; *silita* - \[is\] made; *sayane* - о she whose bed; *harim* - Krsna; *avalokaya* - look at; *saphalaya* - \[and\] make successful; *nayane* - \[your\] eyes.

**Перевод:**

**“O Radha, lying on a bed of moist lotus petals! Look at Krsna and make your eyes successful.**

**“O angry woman...**

**Комментарий:**

\[Radha asks\] “What should I do?”

\[*Sakhi* replies\] “O Radha, lying on a bed of moist lotus petals! Look at Krsna, the jewel of heroes, endowed with all qualities like beauty, and make your eyes successful.” It is said *aksanvatam phalam idam*: \[The *gopls* said,\] “For persons with eyes, we do not know any other success than seeing the face of the younger one who plays the flute” - (SB 10.21.7). You are pained by separation, and lying on a bed of moist lotus petals. By glancing at him affectionately, since he is following you, you’ll become successful. Or, have I not told you many times to look at Krsna lying on a bed of moist lotus petals because of the pain of separation from you? Or, look at Krsna sitting on a cool bed of moist lotus petals to conciliate you.

### Text 9.7

*janayasi manasi kim iti guru-khedam \|*  
*srnu mama vacanam anihita-bhedam \|\|7\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*janayasi* - Do you produce; *manasi* - in \[your\] mind; *kim* - why; *iti* - such; *guru* - intense; *khedam* - suffering? *srnu* - hear; *mama* - my; *vacanam* - words; *anihita* - of \[what is\] disagreeable; *bhedam* - the breaking.

**Перевод:**

**“Why do you grieve so intensely in your mind? Hear my words, which are beneficial.**

**“O angry woman...**

**Комментарий:**

Why do you produce intense suffering in your heart? Hear the words I will speak. The words are against anything disagreeable. They are beneficial for young couples. Or, hear my words, so that there is no difference with me. You should understand, “This woman is my friend. She is not different from me. She does not speak anything to unsettle me.” With this understanding, listen to my words.

### Text 9.8

*harir upayatu vadatu bahu-madhuram \|*  
*kim iti karosi hrdayam atividhuram \|\|8\|\|*

*madhave ma kuru...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*harir* - Krsna; *upayatu* - may \[he\] come; *vadatu* - \[and\] speak; *bahu* - very; *madhuram* - sweetly! *kim -* Why; *iti* - such; *karosi* - do you cause; *hrdayam* - \[your\] heart; *atividhuram* - more suffering?

**Перевод:**

**“May Krsna come! May he speak sweetly! Why do you cause your heart more suffering?**

**“O angry woman...”**

**Комментарий:**

“Krsna should come near you. He should speak very sweetly, or in a pleasing manner. If that is so, why make your heart suffer.”

### Text 9.9

*sri-jayadeva-bhanitam atilalitam \|*  
*sukhayatu rasika-janam hari-caritam \|\|9\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sri-jayadeva -* By Sri Jayadeva; *bhanitam* - described; *atilalitam* - attractive with *srngara-rasa*; *sukhayatu* - may \[they\] delight; *rasika* -  skilled in tasting the \[excellent\] *rasa* \[of *bhakti*\], *janam* - devotees; *hari -* of Krsna; *caritam* - the activities.

**Перевод:**

**May the activities of Krsna filled with attractive srngara-rasa, described by Jayadeva, create joy in the devotees who are skillful at tasting the excellent rasa of bhakti. (END OF SONG EIGHTEEN)**

**Комментарий:**

These activities are descriptions of *srhgara-rasa* and attractive with songs (*atilalitam*).

*srngara-pracura cesta yatra tam lalitam viduh \|\|*

**Where there are activities of explicit conjugal nature it is known as** ***lalitam*****, voluptuousness. (BRS 2.1.267)**

### Text 9.10

*snigdhe уat parusasi yat pranamati stabdhasi yad ragini*  
*dvesasthasi yad unmukhe vimukhatam yatasi tasmin priye \|*  
*tad yuktam viparita-karini tava srikhanda-carca visam*  
*sitamsus tapano himam hutavahah krida-mudo yatanah \|\|10\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*snigdhe* - \[When he is\] gentle; *yat* - since; *parusa* - harsh; *asi* - your are; *yat -* since; *pranamati* - \[when he\] offers respects; *stabdha* - obstinate; *asi* - you are; *yad* - since; *ragini* - \[when he\] shows affection; *dvesa* - in hatred; *stha* - engaged; *asi* - you are; *yad* - \[and\] since; *unmukhe* - \[when he is\] facing \[you\]; *vimukhatam* - to \[a state of\] turning away \[from him\]; *yata* - gone; *asi* - you are; *tasmin* - he; *priye* - \[your\] beloved; *tad* - therefore; *yuktam* - appropriate \[condition\]; *viparita* - opposite; *karini* - о woman who makes; *tava* - your; *srikhanda* - of the sandal tree; *eared* - the paste; *visam* - \[becomes\] poison; *sltamsus* - the moon; *tapano* - \[is\] causing pain; *himam* - snow; *hutavahah* - \[becomes\] fire; *krida* - of pastimes; *mudo* - \[and\] the pleasures; *yatanah* - \[become\] agonies.

**Перевод:**

**\[The sakhi continues,\] “O Radha, you take things in the wrong way! Because of this, everything has happened. Sandalwood becomes poison. The moon gives pain. Snow becomes fire. The pleasure of pastimes becomes agony. This is because, when he speaks gently, you reply harshly. When he offers respect, you do not respond. When he shows affection, you show hatred. When he faces you, you turn and go away.”**

**Комментарий:**

О Radha, who takes things in the opposite way! For this reason (*tat*) everything has happened to you. One who acts in an opposite way has opposite results. What is that?

Sandalwood paste becomes poison, giving pain. And the moon gives pain like the sun, though its nature is cooling. Snow gives pain like fire. Pleasure of playing becomes painful because you use harsh words when your beloved (*snigdhe*) shows affection with sweet words. When he offers respect, you do not respond. When he shows affection, you remain there showing hatred. When he stands before you to embrace you, you turn and go away.

*Yatana* means sharp pain according to *Amara-kosa*. The ornament is called *virodha* (metaphor of contradiction). The poetic meter is *sardula-vikriditam*.

### Text 9.11

*sandrananda-purandaradi-divisad-vrndair amandadarad*  
*anamrair mukutendra-nila-manibhih sandarsitendindiram \|*  
*svacchandam makaranda-sundara-galan-mandakini-*  
*meduram sri-govinda-padaravindam asubha-skandaya vandamahe \|\|11\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sandra* - \[Is\] abundant; *ananda* - whose bliss; *purandara* - \[is\] Indra; *adi* - \[and\] at whose head; *divisad* - of devatas; *vrndair* - with groups; *amandadarad* - out of great respect; *anamrair* - bowing; *mukuta* - on \[their\] crowns; *indra-nila* - \[which are\] sapphires; *manibhih* - with gems; *sandarsita* - \[are\] manifested; *indindiram* - on which bees; *svacchandam* - freely; *makaranda* - \[as\] honey; *sundara* - beautiful; *galan* - flowing; *mandakini* - with the Ganga; *meduram* - attractive; *sri-govinda* - of Sri Govinda \[Krsna\] ; *padaravindam* - to the lotus-feet; *asubha* - of sins \[which obstruct *bhakti*\]; *skandaya* - for the destruction; *vandamahe* - we offer respects.

**Перевод:**

**To destroy sins which obstruct bhakti we offer respects to Govinda’s lotus feet, which are charming, with the Ganga flowing beautifully and freely like honey. They are covered with bees in the form of sapphires from the crowns of blissful devatas headed by Indra offering respects out of great respect.**

**Комментарий:**

The author offers blessings at the end of the chapter.

We offer respects to the lotus feet of Govinda. Why? To destroy sins which are obstacles to *bhakti* (*asubha-skandaya*). Dative case is used to indicate “for a purpose.” Plural (‘we’ offer respects) is used to indicate his students as well. Destroying sins is easily done, for those feet are attractive with the Ganga flowing beautifully and freely like honey. Flowing from his feet, the Ganga purifies the whole world. What then to speak of the purifying power of his feet themselves! There is honey flowing from the lotus which is compared to the Ganga. This is *upama alankara* (simile or metaphor).

The lotus feet seemed to be covered with bees manifested in the form of sapphire crowns, as groups of blissful devatas headed by Indra bow down completely out of great reverence. This illustrates that his feet are worthy of respect. The lotus is described with the feet being lotuses, the Ganga being their honey and the sapphires in the devatas crowns being the bees. The word *mandakini* (flowing steadily) indicates the fame of the Lord since that river flows constantly, spreading out everywhere like his fame. The title of this chapter *Manda-mukunda* which means indolent or diligent Krsna.

## Chapter Ten. Catura-caturbhujah - Channing Krsna

### Text 10.1

*atrantare masrna-rosa-vasam asima-*  
*nihsvasa-nihsaha-mukhim sumukhim upetya \|*  
*savridam iksita-sakhi-vadanam dinante*  
*sananda-gadgada-padam harir ity uvaca \|\|1\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*atra* - Then; *antare* - \[taking\] the opportuniy; *masrna* - mild; *rosa* -of anger; *vasam* - under the influence; *asima* - unlimited; *nihivasa* - through sighing; *nihsaha* - \[was indicated\] intolerable \[suffering\] ; *mukhim* - in whose face; *sumukhim* - to her (Radha) whose face \[was\] beautiful; *upetya* - having approached \[her\]; *savridam* -bashfully; *iksita* - was glanced at; *sakhi* - of \[her\] female friend; *vadanam* - by whom the face; *dinante* - in the evening; *sananda* - in bliss; *gadgada* - stammering; *padam* - with words; *harir* - Krsna; *ity* - as follows; *uvaca* - spoke.

**Перевод:**

**Taking the opportunity, in the evening, Krsna approached Radha, whose beautiful face showed mild anger, indicating her intolerable suffering through a face expressing unlimited sighing, as she glanced at the face of her sakhi shyly. He spoke to her with stammering words in bliss as follows.**

**Комментарий:**

Informed by the *sakhi,* Krsna himself pacifies Radha.

Taking the opportunity of the evening, Krsna approached Radha. Radha had a happy face (*sumukhim*) with some dissipation of her anger. Krsna spoke the following words in bliss, in a stammering voice. Radha has only some mild anger (*masrna-rosa-vasam*) because of her *sakhi’s* words. Her face showed intolerable suffering with unlimited sighs. She looked at the face of her *sakhi* with shyness.

**\|\| Song Nineteen \|\|**

*desavaradi-raga, astatali-tala*

**The song is sung in the** ***raga*** **of** ***desavaradi*** **and in the** ***tala*** **of** ***astatali.***

Epigraph to the song: The characteristics of the *raga* are as follows:

*arabdha-gama-nidra*  
*nighurnayamana-nayana-yugmaghra \|*  
*sukha-sayini nija-dayite*  
*desa-varadi bhaved gauri \|\|*

***Desa-varadi*** **is golden in complexion and is about to sleep. She has her eyes kissed, which are rolling about, while her lover lies down happily.**

*laghur dhruta laghus cety astatali prakirtitah*

***Astatali*** **is 4 (*****laghu*****) + 2 (*****drutam*****) + 4 (*****laghu*****)**

### Text 10.2

*vadasi yadi kincid api danta-ruci-kaumudi*  
*harati dara-timiram atighoram \|*  
*sphurad-adhara-sidhave tava vadana-candrama*  
*rocayatu locana-cakoram \|\|2\|\|*

*priye caru-sile munca mayi manam anidanam \|*  
*sapadi madananalo dahati mama manasam*  
*dehi mukha-kamala-madhu-panam \|\| dhru. \|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vadasi* - You speak; *yadi* - if; *kincid* - a little; *api* - even; *danta* - of \[your\] teeth; *ruci* - of the effulgence; *kaumudi* - the moonlight; *harati* - will dissipate; *dara* - fearful; *timiram* - the darkness; *atighoram* -terrifying; *sphurad* - being revealed; *adhara* - of lips; *sidhave* - for the nectar; *tava* - your; *vadana* - of \[your\] face; *candrama* - the moon; *rocayatu* - \[and\] should cause to long; *locana* - of my eyes; *cakoram* - the cakora bird.

*priye* - О beloved; *caru* - \[is\] agreeable; *site* - whose character; *munca* - give up; *mayi* - towards me; *manam* - anger; *anidanam* - causeless; *sapadi* - At this time; *madana* - of Cupid; *analo* - the fire; *dahati* - burns; *mama* - my; *manasam* - mind; *dehi* - Allow \[me\]; *mukha* - of your face; *kamala* - lotus; *madhu* - of the nectar; *panam* - the drinking.

**Перевод:**

**\[Krsna speaks,\] “If you speak just a little, the moon light effulgence of your teeth will dissipate the fearful, terrifying darkness. The  moon of your face should make my cakora eyes desire the nectar of your lips now being revealed.**

**“O beloved! Give up your causeless anger towards me. The fire of  Cupid torments my mind at this time. Let me drink the nectar of  your lotus face. (chorus)**

**Комментарий:**

\[Krsna says,\] “Give up your anger, which prevents our amorous enjoyment.”

\[Radha asks,\] “What is it to you if I have anger?”

“As soon as you show anger (*sapadi*) the fire of Cupid pains my mind. Therefore, where is the question of even talking about giving up your anger? Give me the opportunity to drink the honey of your lotus face in order to alleviate the pain that has arisen because of your anger. ” He requests to see her face in front of him, which he cannot see now. “Give the opportunity to me, who am completely dedicated to you (*caru-sile*). Therefore, give up your anger which has no cause (*anidanam*)." Or, he requests in pain, “Please immediately (*sapadi*) let me hear the nectar of your words flowing from your lotus mouth.” Drinking often means hearing. Or, “let me drink the nectar of your lips in your lotus mouth.”

О beloved! If you speak a little, neither affectionate nor unaffectionate, then the moon light of the effulgence of your teeth will remove the fearful, terrifying darkness. May the moon of your face generate desire for the nectar of your lips, now displayed to the *cakora* bird of my eyes. *Sidhave* (nectar) is in the dative to express purpose. This indicates that the moon of her face will satisfy his eyes. It is indicated that his eyes obtain their life from seeing her, just as the *cakora* attains life from the moonlight.

### Text 10.3

*satyam evasi yadi sudati mayi kopini*  
*dehi khara-nakhara-sara-ghatam \|*  
*ghataya bhuja-bandhanam janaya rada-khandanam*  
*yena va bhavati sukha-jatam \|\|3\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*satyam* - Really; *eva* - indeed; *asi* - you are; *yadi* - if; *sudati* - о woman \[Radha\] with shining teeth; *mayi* - with me; *kopini* - angry; *dehi* -grant \[me\]; *khara* - sharp; *nakhara* - of \[your\] fingernails; *sara* - with the arrows; *ghatam* - the beating; *ghataya* - bring about; *bhuja* - in \[your\] arms; *bandhanam* - the tying up \[of me\]; *janaya* - \[and\] produce; *rada* - with \[your\] teeth; *khandanam* - the biting \[of my lip\]; *yena* - by which means; *va* - indeed; *bhavati* - arises; *sukha* - of \[your\] happiness; *jatam* - the production.

**Перевод:**

**“O Radha with shining teeth! If you are really angry with me, beat me with the arrows of your sharp fingernails, tie me up in your arms, and bite my lip with your teeth so that you can be completely happy.**

**“Give up your causeless anger...**

**Комментарий:**

He addresses her, as she shows false anger. О Radha with shining teeth! If you are really angry with me, beat me with the arrows of your sharp fingernails. If that does not satisfy you, tie me up in your arms. If that is not enough, bite my lip with your teeth (*rada*), so that you can be completely happy. Punishing me, suitable to my offense, give up your anger. On the pretext of obtaining punishment for his offense, he requests that she scratch him with her nails, embrace him and kiss him.

### Text 10.4

*tvam asi mama jivanam tvam asi mama bhusanam*  
*tvam asi mama bhava-jaladhi-ratnam \|*  
*bhavatu bhavatiha mayi satatam anurodhini*  
*tatra mama hrdayam atiyatnam \|\|4\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*tvam* - You; *asi* - are; *mama* - my; *jivanam* - life; *tvam* - You; asi - are; *mama* - my; *bhusanam* - ornament; *tvam* - You; *asi* - are; *mama* - my; *bhava* - of Vraja (or of existence); *jaladhi* - in the ocean; *ratnam* - gem; *bhavatu* - May be; *bhavati* - your honor; *iha* - now; *mayi* - to me; *satatam* - always; *anurodhim* - favorable; *tatra* - since; *mama* -my; *hrdayam* - heart; *atiyatnam* - makes the utmost effort \[to favor you\].

**Перевод:**

**“You are my life. You are my ornament. You are my gem mined from the ocean of Vraja. You should always favor me, since my heart makes the utmost effort to favor you.**

**“Give up your causeless anger...**

**Комментарий:**

“Let the woman whom you love punish you. Why should I do it?”

You are my life. If you are unhappy, I am unhappy. If you are happy, I am happy. In this way you are my life. What is more pleasing than one’s life? You are dearest to me. You are my ornament. How can I, with blackish body, have beauty from any ornament, without you, endowed with a golden body? This indirectly indicates his request for her embrace. You are my jewel in the ocean of Vraja, in the ocean of birth among the cowherds. After entering the ocean and attaining a jewel with effort, one becomes perfect. I become perfect after attaining you with effort. Having attained the jewel among women in Vraja, I am successful. This statement also suggests the great respect of being born in the family of cowherds, being successful by that birth and that, in the ocean of Vraja, there are many jewels of women but Radha is the greatest gem. It is said that the best jewel comes from the ocean.

You (*bhavati*) should always be favorable (*anurodhim*) to me, since I am favorable (*iha*). My heart, my complete *antahkarana*, makes all efforts to be favorable to you (*tatra*). It is engaged in efforts to worship you. *Anuradha* means obedient according to *Amara-kosa*.

### Text 10.5

*nila-nalinabham api tanvi tava locanam*  
*dharayati kokanada-rupam \|*  
*kusuma-sara-bana-bhavena yadi ranjayasi*  
*krsnam idam etad-апиruрат \|\|5\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*nila* - Blue; *nalina* - \[is that\] of the lotus; *abham* - whose color; *api* - even; *tanvi* - о slender woman \[Radha\]; *tava* - your; *locanam* - eyes; *dharayati* - possess; *kokanada* - of the red lotus; *rupam* - the beauty; *kusuma-sara* - Of Cupid (he whose arrows are flowers); *bana* - from the arrows; *bhavena* - by the sentiment of love; *yadi* - if; *ranjayasi* - you re-color; *krsnam* - \[your\] black \[eyes\]; *idam* - that; *etad* - for them \[your eyes\]; *anurupam* - \[would be\] suitable.

**Перевод:**

**“O slender Radha! Your blue lotus eyes have become red lotuses. If you recolor your blackish eyes by sentiments of love from Cupid’s arrows, that is suitable.**

**“Give up your causeless anger...**

**Комментарий:**

О slender Radha! Your eyes with luster like the blue lotus should possess the beauty of red lotuses. If you recolor your black eyes by the desire in Cupid’s arrows, this would be appropriate for your eyes. The eyes of women become red when agitated by Cupid’s arrows. Arrows always become red by the blood staining them, on piercing someone’s body. Giving up anger, you should make your eyes red, ready for enjoyment in love. A thin body indicates her anger. She should give up that anger.

Or, if you colored your black eyes with experiences of *srngara-rasa* (*bhava*) through loving glances (*kusuma-sara-bana*), it would be suitable. If you attract me who is desiring those glances, with your glances, this attraction would be proper for you or proper for Cupid’s arrows. Since you are capable of changing black to red, it is proper that you color me with your glances.

### Text 10.6

*sphuratu kuca-kumbhayor upari mani-manjari*  
*ranjayatu tava hrdaya-desam \|*  
*rasatu rasanapi tava ghana-jaghana-mandale*  
*ghosayatu manmatha-nidesam \|\|6\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sphuratu* - May \[it\] tremble; *kuca-kumbhayor* - \[your\] breasts; *upari* - on; *mani* - of pearls; *manjari* - the necklace; *ranjayatu* - May it color \[with its rays\]; *tava* - your; *hrdaya* - of chest; *desam* - the region; *rasatu* - May \[they\] jingl; *rasana* - the bells; *api* - even; *tava* - your; *ghana* - fleshy; *jaghana* - of hips; *mandale* - on the circle; *ghosayatu* - May \[they\] proclaim; *manmatha* - of the god of love; *nidesam* - the order.

**Перевод:**

**“May the pearl necklace on your breasts tremble. May your chest become beautiful with its rays. May the bells on your fleshy hips jingle and proclaim the order of Cupid.**

**“Give up your causeless anger...**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing that Radha was somewhat pleased, he again speaks.

“May the pearl necklace tremble (*sphuratu*) on your pot-like breasts. May it color your chest with its rays (*ranjayatu*). just as the necklace colors your chest, your lotus eyes on becoming red should change my color, though I am now black. That is proper.” Full pots are decorated with buds, for auspiciousness at the beginning of love sports. This is compared to the breasts decorated with a pearl necklace. “May the bells on your fleshy hips jingle. May they proclaim loudly the order of Cupid. May the bells engage the players in the battle of love on the order of Cupid!” These clever statements also indicate his request that she take the dominant position in love.

### Text 10.7

*sthala-kamala-ganjanam mama hrdaya-ranjanam*  
*janita-rati-ranga-parabhagam \|*  
*bhana masrna-vani karavani carana-dvayam*  
*sarasa-lasad-alaktaka-ragam \|\|7\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*sthala* - \[Growing\] on land; *kamala* - than lotuses; *ganjanam* - more attractive; mama - my; *hrdaya* - in heart; *ranjanam* - producing attraction; *janita* - \[is\] produced; *rati* - of love-making; *ranga* - in the field; *parabhagam* - by which mutual beauty, *bhana* - Give the order; *masrna* - pleasant \[words\]; *vani* - о woman \[Radha\] speaking! *karavani* - Let me make; *carana* - of \[your\] feet; *dvayam* - the pair; *sarasa* - \[our\] *srngara-rasa* (or love); *lasad* - to indicate; *alaktaka* -of lac; *ragam* - the color.

**Перевод:**

**“O Radha, speaking pleasant words! Give the order. Let me decorate your feet with beautiful lac to indicate our love. Those feet are more attractive than lotuses growing on land, they produce attraction in my heart, and stimulate our mutual feelings during love.**

**“Give up your causeless anger...**

**Комментарий:**

О Radha speaking affectionate words! Please give the order (*bhana*). I color your feet with beautiful lac to convey *Srngara-rasa* (*sarasam*). Those feet defeat the beauty of lotuses growing on land instead of water (since her feet are placed on the ground), by their redness, fragrance and softness. This means her feet are more cooling than the lotus since they can pacify the suffering heart. Those feet produce attachment in my heart (*hrdaya-ranjanam*). Or, those feet make colorful stains of lac on my chest in certain lovemaking positions. I accept that.

Those feet produce beauty in the couple in love-making or they produce beauty in attachment to love-making. They act as a stimulus for love. The stains on his chest during love-making also indicate mutual beauty in the couple. *Para-bhaga* means mutual beauty. *Ranga* means skill and attachment according to *Dharani.*

### Text 10.8

*smara-garala-khandanam mama sirasi mandanam*  
*dehi pada-pallavam udaram \|*  
*jvalati mayi daruno madana-kadanaruno*  
*haratu tad-upahita-vikaram \|\|8\|\|*

*priye caru-sile...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*smara* - Of Cupid; *garala* - the poison; *khandanam* - destroying; *mama* - my; *sirasi* - on head; *mandanam* - \[as\] a decoration; *dehi* -put; *pada* - of \[your\] feet; *pallavam* - the buds; *udaram* - beautiful; *jvalati* - Causes pain; *mayi* - to me; *daruno* - intolerable; *madana* - \[arising\] from Cupid; *kadanaruno* - the sun’s pain; *haratu* - May \[your feet\] remove; *tad* - by it; *upahita* - caused; *vikaram* - the \[adverse\] effect!

**Перевод:**

**“Put the buds of your beautiful feet, which destroy the snake poison of Cupid, on my head, making them my decoration. The sun’s pain, caused by Cupid, intolerable to me, gives me pain. May those feet remove those effects!**

**“Give up your causeless anger...”**

**Комментарий:**

О beloved! Put the beautiful (*udaram*) buds of your feet on my head. This indicates that her feet are worthy of worship since the Lord has such respect for them. Her feet are compared to buds since they are soft, fragrant, take away pain, are auspicious (like sprouts), and beautiful. *Udaram* also means they bestow desires (being generous). Thus they are worthy of service. Those feet decorate my head. They destroy the poison (*garala*) of Cupid. Cupid is called a snake because the Malaya wind produces pain for the separated lover, which causes fainting. By requesting Radha to place her feet on his head he also criticizes her hard Garuda-like nature. Garuda destroys a snake’s poison by striking it with his feet, but she must relieve him of pain by placing her soft feet on his head.

The sun’s heat arising from Cupid, intolerable to me, inflicts pain. May your feet remove the effects such as fatigue. The heat of the sun is alleviated by putting buds on the head. *Aruna* means the sun and *kadana* means pain.

### Text 10.9

*iti catula-catu-patu-caru mura-vairino*  
*radhikam adhi vacana-jatam \|*  
*jayati jayadeva-kavi-bharati-bhusitam*  
*manini-jana-janita-satam \|\|9\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*iti* - Thus; *catula* - attractive; *catu* - pleasing; *patu* - skillful; *caru* -\[and\] soft; *mura* - of Mura; *vairino* - of the slayer (Krsna); *radhikam* - Radha; *adhi* - to; *vacana* - of the words; *jatam* - the aggregate; *jayati* - may \[it\] remain \[with excellence\]; *jayadeva* - Jayadeva; *kavi* - of the poet; *bharati* - by the eloquence; *bhusitam* - ornamented; *manini* - respectable; *jana -* for people; *janita* - \[is\] produced; *satam* - by which joy.

**Перевод:**

**May all the attractive, pleasing, skilful and soft words spoken by Krsna to Radha, ornamented by the eloquence words of Jayadeva, bestowing joy to respectable people, remain with excellence. (END OF SONG NINETEEN)**

**Комментарий:**

In this way, may all the words that Krsna spoke to Radha remain with all excellence (*jayati*)! Those words are attractive (*catula*), pleasing (*catu*), skilful (*patu*) and soft (*caru*). They are ornamented by the words (*bharati*) of Jayadeva. These words should produce happiness (*satam*) for respectable people.

Another version is *jayati padmavati-ramana-jayadeva-kavi-bharati-bhanitam atisatam.* May these words which produce great joy (*atisatam*), remain excellent. These are described by Jayadeva, who is eloquent as Sarasvati and who is dear to his wife Padmvati. In this case *bharati* cannot mean words since “described by words” (*bharatya bhanitam*) would be redundant. In *Rasarnava-sudharkara* it is said *srngaradisu sarvesu rasesv istaiva bharati*: there is eloquence in all *rasas* such as *srhgara*. It is said in (*Srngara-tilaka*):

*pradhana-purusa-praya sad-vakrokti-nirantara \|*  
*bharatiyam bhaved vrttir vira-hasyadbhutasraya \|\|*

**Eloquence occurs in sentences with frequent crooked words, spoken by a prominent person expressing valor, comedy and astonishment. (*****Srngara-tilaka*** **3.44)**

*Catula* means beautiful according to *Dharani.* *Catu* means pleasing words according to *Hadda-candra.* *Patta* means clever according to *Visva.* *Sata* means happiness according to *Amara-kosa.*

### Text 10.10

*parihara krtatanke sankam tvaya satatam ghana-*  
*stana-jaghanayakrante svante paranavakasini \|*  
*visati vitanor anyo dhanyo na ko’pi mamantaram*  
*pranayini parirambharambhe vidhehi vidheyatam \|\|10\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

parihara - Give up; *krta* - \[is\] performed; *atanke* - о woman (Radha) by whom anger; *sankam* - the doubt \[that I have enjoyed with another woman\]; *tvaya* - by you; *satatam* - always; *ghana* - \[are\] full; *stana* - whose breasts; *jaghanaya* - \[and\] hips; *akrante* - filled; *svante* - \[my\] heart; *para* - for others; *anavakasini* - \[since it\] has no opportunity; *visati* - enters; *vitanor* - because of Cupid or love; *anyo* - other; *dhanyo* - fortunate (woman); *na* - no; *ko’pi* - person; *mama* - my; *antaram* - heart; *pranayini* - о beloved; *parirambha* -of embracing \[me\]; *arambhe* - for the beginning; *vidhehi -* do; *vidheyatam* - what must be done.

**Перевод:**

**\[Krsna continues,\] “O angry Radha! Give up your doubts about me! No other woman can enter my heart which is filled continually with you who is endowed with full breasts and hips, since my heart has no opportunity for others. О beloved! Do what should be done in order to begin embracing me.**

**Комментарий:**

Having shown that he has no fault, Krsna now becomes eager for enjoyment.

“O angry Radha! Give up the doubt that I have enjoyed with another woman because, another fortunate person, another woman, does not dwell in my heart because of love (*vitanoh*).”

“Why?”

“It is devoid of other opportunities. It is filled continually by you with your full breasts and hips. No one else can enter my heart since it is filled by you at all times. Persons with limbs cannot enter but a person without limbs, Cupid, can enter. О beloved! Do what must be done to start embracing!” *Atanka* means doubt or anger according to *Dharani.*

### Text 10.11

*mugdhe vidhehi mayi nirdaya-danta-damsa-*  
*dor-valli-bandha-nivida-stana-pidanani \|*  
*candi tvam eva madam udvaha panca-bana-*  
*candala-kanda-dalanad asavah prayantu \|\|11\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*mugdhe* - О beautiful woman \[Radha\]; *vidhehi* - give; *mayi* - to me; *nirdaya* - merciless; *danta* - of \[your\] teeth; damsa - the biting; *dor* -of \[your\] arms; *valli* - of the creepers; *bandha* - the binding; *nivida* - firm; *stana* - of \[your\] breasts; *pidanani* - \[and\] the squeezing; *candi* - О most angry woman \[Radha\]; *tvam* - you; *eva* - only; *mudam* -pleasure \[by punishing me\]; *udvaha* - take; *panca-bana* - \[Who is\] Cupid; *candala* - of *candala* (or of beastly); *kanda* - \[caused\] by the arrows; *dalanad* - because of the carnage; *asavah* - \[my\] life breaths; *prayantu* - let \[them\] leave.

**Перевод:**

**“O beautiful Radha! Give me pain by biting with your merciless teeth, by binding me with the creepers of your arms, and squeezing me with your firm breasts. О most angry one! Take pleasure in this! May my life airs leave because of carnage caused by the arrows of beastly Cupid.**

**Комментарий:**

“If you do not agree to this, then punish me suitable to my offense.

О beautiful Radha (*mugdhe*)! Or, О foolish Radha! Give me pain with your merciless teeth by biting, by the creepers of your arms by binding, by your full breasts through squeezing me. О most angry Radha (*candi*)! Take pleasure (*mudam*) by punishing me, since I have offended you. May my life airs leave my body, being struck by the arrows of Cupid, who is a candala outcaste because he wounds innocent people. It is not proper to be struck by a candala. It is better that you punish me. It is suggested by his words that, having suffered up to death by the pain of love, he asks her for mercy in the form of enjoyment. *Candi* means very anger according to *Amara-kosa*.

### Text 10.12

*sasi-mukhi tava bhati bhangura-bhrur*  
*yuva-jana-moha-karala-kala-sarpi \|*  
*tad-udita-bhaya-bhanjanaya уunam*  
*tvad-adhara-sidhu-sudhaiva siddha-mantrah \|\|12\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

sasi - \[Is like\] the moon; *mukhi* - О woman whose face; tava - your; *bhati* - shines; bhangura - crooked; *bhrur* - brow; yuva - young; *jana* - of people; *moha* - for the delusion; *karala* - cruel; kala - black; *sarpi* - \[as\] a snake; *tad* - By it \[the snake\]; *udita* - produced; *bhaya* - the fear; *bhanjanaya* - for destroying; *уunam tvad* - your; *adhara* - lips; *sidhu-sudha* - \[is\] the inebriating nectar; *iva siddha* - effective; *mantrah* - the mantra.

**Перевод:**

**“O moon-faced Radha! Your crooked brow shines like a cruel black snake that bewitched youths. The effective mantra to destroy the fear that this snake causes, is the inebriating nectar of your lips.**

**Комментарий:**

\[Radha says\] “But I am not angry.”

To this Krsna replies. “O Radha with a moon-like face! Your crooked brow is glorious. It is the terrifying (*karala*) female black snake for bewildering young people. The snake by its glance can bewilder. Thus just by your glance you bewilder people. That is most terrifying. To destroy the fear that arises from this snake, for youths like me, the effective mantra is the special inebriating (*sidhu*) nectar of your lips. If you let someone drink the nectar of your lips, the poison from the snake will be counteracted. Therefore, by letting me drink your lips, give me life since I have been bitten by the snake of your anger.”

### Text 10.13

*vyathayati vrtha maunam tanvi prapancaya pancamam*  
*taruni madhuralapais tapam vinodaya drstibhih \|*  
*sumukhi vimukhi-bhavam tavad vimunca na munca mam*  
*svayam atisaya-snigdho mugdhe priyo’yam upasthitah \|\|13\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*vyathayati* - Pains \[me\]; *vrtha* - uselessly; *maunam* - \[your\] silence; *tanvi* - о slender woman \[Radha\]; *prapancaya* - Sing; *pancamam* - the *pancama raga*; *taruni* - О young woman \[Radha\]; *madhura* - sweet; *alapais* - with \[your\] words; *tapam* - \[my\] suffering; *vinodaya* - dissipate; *drstibhih* - \[and\] with \[your\] glances; *sumukhi* - О beautiful-faced woman \[Radha\]; *vimukhi* - of indifference; *bhavam* - \[your\] condition; *tavad* - so long; *vimunca* - give up; *na* - Not; *munca* - do give up; *mam* - me; *svayam* - \[On his\] own; *atisaya* - \[is\] great; *snigdho* - whose affection; mugdhe - о beautiful woman (Radha); *priyo* - \[your\] lover; *ayam* - this; *upasthitah* - \[has\] come.

**Перевод:**

**“O thin-bodied Radha! Your useless silence pains me. Sing the pancama-raga. О young woman! Dissipate my suffering by your glance and sweet words. О beautiful-faced woman! Give up your long indifference. Do not give me up. О beautiful woman! Your lover, full of affection, has come on his own.**

**Комментарий:**

When she does not answer, he again speaks. О thin-bodied Radha! Your silence, your useless refusal to speak, gives me pain. You should not maintain anger this long. You have become thin. This is the significance of addressing her as thin-waisted. Sing the *pancama-raga*. О young woman! Remove my pain by your sweet words or the sweet *alapa* in singing. О Radha with beautiful face! Give up your indifference (*vimukhi-bhavam*) by giving a glance from the corner of your eyes. Addressing her as *sumukhi* indicates that her indifference (*vimukhi*) is not proper. О beautiful woman! Your lover has come without being called. He is full of the greatest affection. Therefore, give up your anger. It is not proper to reject a person who has come on his own.

### Text 10.14

*bandhuka-dyuti-bandhavo’yam adharah snigdho madhuka-cchavir*  
*gandas candi cakasti nila-nalina-sri-mocanam locanam \|*  
*nasabhyeti tila-prasuna-padavim kundabha-danti priye*  
*prayas tvan-mukha-sevaya vijayate visvam sa puspayudhah \|\|14\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*bandhuka* - of the *bandhuka* flower; *dyuti* - of the splendor; *bandhavo* - \[is\] the friend; *ayam* - this; *adharah* - \[your\] lip; *snigdho* - tender; *madhuka* - of the madhuka flower; *cchavir* - \[as\] the beauty; *gandas* - \[your\] cheek; *candi* - о cruel woman (Radha); *cakasti* - shines; *nila* -blue; *nalina* - of the lotus; *sri* - the beauty; *mocanam* - \[is\] rejecting; *locanam* - \[your\] eye; *nasa* - \[and your\] nose; *abhyeti* - surpasses; *tila* - of sesame; *prasuna* - of the flower; *padavim* - the fame; *kunda* - Of the jasmine flower; *abha* - whose color \[is that\]; *danti* - having teeth; *priye* - о beloved (Radha); *prayas* - generally; *tvan* - your; *mukha* - to face; *sevaya* - by the service; *vijayate* - conquers; *visvam* - the world; *sa* - the; *puspayudhah* - flower-armed one (the Cupid).

**Перевод:**

**“Your lip is the friend of the beauty of the bandhuka flower. Your tender cheek shines with the beauty of the madhuka flower. О cruel woman! Your eye rejects the beauty of the blue lotus. Your nose surpasses the fame of the sesame flower. О Radha, having teeth whose color is that of the jasmine flower! О beloved! Cupid, with his flower weapons conquers the world by the service of your face.**

**Комментарий:**

He begins to praise her body in order to remove her anger. О angry Radha! О beloved! Your lips are the friend of the glow of the red *bandhuka* flower. This means they are better than the *bandhuka* flower. Your tender cheeks shine the color of the *madhuka* flower— pale because of separation from me. Your eye rejects the beauty of the blue lotus by its greater beauty. The natural blackness of the pupil of the eye without mascara surpasses the blue lotus. О Radha with teeth the color of the white *kunda* flower! Your nose attains (*abhyeti*) the fame of the pink sesame flower. Another version has *atyeti* (surpasses). I speculate (*prayah*) that Cupid, the holder of the flower weapon, has conquered the world by the service of your face. He usually conquers the world with his five arrows. He conquers the world, having obtained the *bandhuka, madhuka*, blue lotus, sesame and *kunda* (jasmine) flowers from your face. They are red, yellow, blue, pink and white. The arrows of Cupid attract, control, madden, melt and dry up. Another version has *tvan-mukha-senaya* (by the glance on your face) instead of *tvan-mukha-sevaya* (by the service of your face). *Bandhuka* is the *bandhujiva* flower (red). *Madhuka* is also called *madhupuspa* according to *Haravali.*

### Text 10.15

*drsau tava madalase vadanam indu-maty-aspadam*  
*gatir jana-manorama vidhuta-rambham urи-dvayam \|*  
*ratis tava kalavati rucira-citra-lekhe bhruvav*  
*aho vibudha-yauvatam vahasi tanvi prthvi-gata \|\|15\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*drsau* - Eyes; *tava* - your; *mada* - with joy; *alase* - \[are\] languid; *vadanam* - \[Your\] face; *indu* - \[which is like\] the moon; *maty* - of thought; *aspadam* - \[is\] the abode; *gatir* - \[Your\] gait; *jana* - to \[all\] people; *manorama* - \[is\] attractive; *vidhuta* - \[Is\] surpassed; *rambham* - \[is that\] by which the banana; *uru* - of \[your\] thighs; *dvayam* - the pair; *ratis* - Pastimes of love; *tava* - your; *kalavati* -\[are\] skilled \[in the arts of love\]; *rucira* - Attractive; *citra-lekhe* - \[are\] paintings; *bhruvav* - \[your\] brows, *aho* - Ah; *vibudha* - of young goddesses; *yauvatam* - the assemblage; *vahasi* - you carry \[within you\]; *tanvi* - о slender woman \[Radha\]; *prthvi* - on earth; *gata* - \[thought\] present.

**Перевод:**

**“Your eyes are languid with joy. Your face is comparable to the moon. Your gait is attractive to all people. Your two thighs surpass the banana. Your pastimes of love are most skillful. Your two brows are attractive paintings. Ah! Slender Radha! Though present on earth, you carry within you all the young goddesses.”**

**Комментарий:**

О thin Radha! Though living on earth, you contain all the young women *devatas.* How astonishing (*aho*)! Just by one of your limbs you become a *devata* woman, and deride the beauty of all the *devata* women. This is explained. Your eyes are languid with joy (*madalase*). In Svarga there is one woman called Madalasa. You have two eyes as two Madalasas. How astonishing! Your face is thought to be the moon (*indu-mati*). You have more beauty in your face than the beauty of the *devata* woman Indumati. Your gait is attractive (*manorama*) to people like me. That gait is non-different from the *devata* woman named Manorama or Laksmi. The beauty of Laksmi is included in your gait. Your two thighs defeat the banana (*rambham*) by their beauty. The beauty of your thighs defeats the *devata* woman Rambha. Your love play is skilled (*kalavati*) in the arts of Cupid, possessing *hava, bhava, kilakincita, mottayita* and *bibboka*. Your skill in love defeats that of the *devata* woman named Kalavati. Your two brows are two paintings (*citra-lekhe*) which have been produced (*racita*). Instead of *racita* sometimes *rucira* (attractive) is seen. There is one Citralekha in Svarga. You have two Citralekhas in your brows. This is astonishing. The ornament is *preyas* (flattery). *Dandi* explains this. *Prey ah priyatarakhyanam rasavad rasa-pesalam*: *preyah* is a statement overburdened with affection. The poetic meter is *prthvi-namaka* as hinted in the verse. Two verses have described the acts of pacifying the angry woman.

### Text 10.16

*pritim vas tanutam harih kuvalayapidena sardham rane*  
*radha-plna-payodhara-smarana-krt-kumbhena sambhedavan \|*  
*yatra svidyati mllati ksanam atha ksipte dvipe tat-ksanat*  
*kamsasyalam abhuj jitam jitam iti vyamoha-kolahalah \|\|16\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*pritim* - Bliss; *vas* - upon you \[the singers and listeners of the poem\]; *tanutam* - may \[he\] bestow; *harih* - Krsna; *kuvalayapidena* - The elephant Kuvalayapida; *sardham* - together with; *rane* - in battle; *radha* - of Radha; *pina* - full; *payodhara* - of the breasts; *smarana* - memories; *krt* - produced; *kumbhena* - whose forehead-protrusions; *sambhedavan* - meeting; *yatra* - at the time when; *svidyati* - \[he\] sweats; *milati* - \[and\] closes \[his\] eyes; *ksanam* - for a moment; *atha* - \[and\] then; *ksipte* - \[ when it\] was thrown down; *dvipe* - the elephant; *tat-ksanat* - at that moment; *kamsasyalam abhuj* - there arose; *jitam* - “\[Kamsa has been\] defeated; *jitam* - \[Kamsa has been\] defeated”; *iti* - thus; *vyamoha* - along with fainting; *kolahalah* - an uproar.

**Перевод:**

**May Krsna bestow bliss to the singers and listeners. When Krsna met the elephant Kuvalayapida in battle, its forehead protrusions reminded him of Radha’s full breasts, and at that time he began perspiring and closed his eyes for a moment, but then on his killing the elephant there arose uproar along with fainting as the crowd exclaimed “Kamsa has been defeated.”**

**Комментарий:**

The poet offers blessings at the end of the chapter.

May Krsna spread bliss to all the singers and listeners of this poem! Krsna is described as follows. He united with the elephant Kuvalayapida, whose forehead protrusions made him remember the full breasts of Radha during the battle. This indicates his absorption in *srngara-rasa* and the high position of Radha in his mind. Here Krsna’s astonishing heroism is described along with the manifestation of *srngara-rasa* while fighting with a huge elephant which generally causes fear—which is contrary to *srngara-rasa*. In that battle, he began perspiring from *sattvika-bhavas* stimulated by seeing the elephant’s protrusions (thinking of Radha’s breasts). He closed his eyes for a moment out of bliss, and then when the elephant was killed (*ksipte*), there was an uproar and fainting. “Kamsa has been completely (*alam*) defeated. He has been defeated.” The repetition is to express confusion.

The meaning is this: first, joy arose among the followers of Kamsa when Krsna perspired and closed his eyes and later, when they saw that the elephant was killed by the enemy, they fainted (*vyamoha*) and uproar arose among them “Ah! Ah!”. Or, on seeing Krsna’s *sattvika-bhavas*, by mistake they clamored, “He (Krsna) has been defeated.”

## Chapter Eleven. Sananda-govindah - Blissful Krsna

### Text 11.1

*suciram anunayena prinayitva mrgaksim*  
*gatavati krta-vese kesave kunja-sayyam \|*  
*racita-rucira-bhusam drsti-mose pradose*  
*sphurati niravasadam kapi radham jagada \|\|1\|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*suciram* - For a long time; *anunayena* - with conciliation; *prinayitva* - having pacified; *mrgaksirh* - the doe-eyed woman \[Radha\]; *gatavati* - \[when he\] went; *krta* - \[was\] done; *vese* - by whom dressing; *kesave* - Kesava \[Krsna\]; *kunja* - in the bower; *sayyam* - to the bed; *racita* - \[were\] arranged; *rucira* - attractive; *bhusam* - on whom ornaments; *drsti* - to vision; *mose* - the impediment; *pradose* - evening; *sphurati* - \[and when it\] was appearing; *niravasadam* - \[and\] whose grief \[was\] gone; *kapi* - a certain \[*sakhi*\]; *radham* - to Radha; *jagada* - spoke.

**Перевод:**

**One sakhi spoke to Radha, now attractively ornamented, her grief dissipated, as evening appeared and impeded the vision, and as Krsna, dressing up, went to the bed in the bower after pacifying her for a long time.**

**Комментарий:**

The tenth chapter described Radha’s anger. This chapter describes Krsna’s meeting with her after she has been respected. Some unnamed sakhi spoke to Radha, as evening appeared, blinding the vision. This made it impossible to see anything. This dense darkness was suitable to produce *rasa*. At this time Krsna was going to the bed within the cottage surrounded by creepers, after pacifying Radha with conciliation (*anunayena*) for a long time. This indicates that he was under her control, having great affection for her. The conciliation consisted of offering flowers and ornaments in order to show that she was his only object of love. Radha had decorated herself with attractive ornaments. She no longer suffered from his not coming (*niravasadam*) since he had pacified her.

**\|\| Song Twenty \|\|**

*vasanta-raga-yati-talabhyam glyate \|*

**The song is sung in the** ***raga*** **of** ***vasanta*** **and in the** ***tala*** **of** ***yati.***

Epigraph to the song: The *sakhi* speaks through this song. The *raga* is *vasanta* and the *tala* is *yati.*

### Text 11.2

*viracita-catu-vacana-racanam*  
*carane racita-pranipatam \|*  
*samprati manjula-vanjula-simani*  
*keli-sayanam anuyatam \|\|2\|\|*

*mugdhe ! madhu-mathanam anugatam anusara radhike \|\| dhru. \|\|*

**Пословный перевод:**

*viracita* - \[Is\] made \[to you\]; *catu* - sweet; *vacana* - of words; *racanam* - by whom the composition; *carane* - at \[your\] feet; *racita* - \[is\] performed; *pranipatam* - by whom prostration; *samprati* - now; *manjula* - attractive; *vanjula* - of the *asoka* creepers; *simani* - at the edge; *keli* - of pastime; *sayanam* - to the bed; *anuyatam* - having gone.

*mugdhe* - О beautiful woman (Radha); *madhu* - Love (*madhurya-rasa*); *mathanam* - stirring up; *anugatam* - \[and\] loyal; *anusara* - follow \[Krsna\]; *radhike* - о Radha!

**Перевод:**

**\[The sakhi speaks,\] “He composes sweet words, bows to your  feet, and has now gone to the pastime bed at the edge of the  attractive asoka creepers.**

**“О beautiful Radha! Follow Krsna, who inspires love and is loyal  to you. (chorus)**

**Комментарий:**

О beautiful Radha (*mugdhe*)! You bewilder the son of Nanda by  your beauty, which is superior to that of all the women of Vraja. Or,  О you are foolish (*mugdhe*) since you delay in following after Krsna who is obedient to you.

О Radha (*radhe*)! You have brought Krsna under control by your  sweet words and the fifth note. Ra can mean sound or form.  \[Thus *radhike* can be analyzed as *ra* (sound) + *adhike* (profuse)\]

*krsna-sara-hara-pancama-svare*  
*munca gita-kutukani radhike \|*  
*preksate’tra harinanudhavitam*  
*tvam nayavad atibhisano janah \|\|*

**\[Visakha says,\] “O Radha, possessing the fifth note which steals the self-control of Krsna! Stop singing that attractive song because your angry husband will see you being pursued by Krsna.” (*****Ujjvala-nilamani*** **4.26)**

He stirs up *madhurya-rasa* (*madhu-mathanam*). This indicates he is  skillful at tasting *srngara-rasa*. Or, it means a bee. Like a bee, Krsna  is greedy to taste Radha’s *rasa*. Or, Krsna is he who agitates spring—  Cupid. Follow after this Cupid. He is obedient (*anugatam*), attracted  by your beauty. Or, he follows you with a favorable attitude.

Or ra means love. О Radha, with extreme love (*ra* (love) + *adhike* (profuse), with natural *srngara-rasa*! *madhum atha nama nu gatam*: “Follow after your beloved who has received (*gatam*) the appointment time (*nu*), after controlling (*nama*) spring (*madhum*) in all ways (*atha*). Follow him after being inclined to play in many ways.” Spring is the *uddipana* (stimulus) for playing.

Or, “O Radha! Follow him.”

How can I go, uncalled?

“The clown called Madhu has come. Offering respects to him (*madhum atha nama*), follow after Krsna.” That is the chorus.

The verse then describes how Krsna is devoted (*anugatam*). He is able to make compositions (r*acanam*) of pleasing words: “You are my beloved worth a million lives. All the women of Vraja are not equal to the gleam of your toe nail. I am controlled by your extraordinary qualities. I cannot remain without you for a moment.”

The word *racita* indicates offering respects along with various types of pacification. He offers respects to your feet. Or, he has offered respects to my feet to pacify you. Or, he has offered respects to my feet in order to go towards the appointed meeting place.

Where is he now? He has gone towards the pastime bed at the edge of the attractive (*manjula*) asoka creepers (*vanjula*). Standing there he waits for you. The creepers are an *uddipana* (stimulus) for love.

### Text 11.3

*ghana-jaghana-stana-bhara-bhare*  
*dara-manthara-carana-viharam \|*  
*mukharita-mani-manjiram upaihi*  
*vidhehi marala-nikaram \|\|3\|\|*

*mugdhe ! madhu-mathanam...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*ghana* - Heavy; *jaghana* - of hips; *stana* - \[and\] breasts; *bhara* - the weight; *bhare* - о woman bearing! *dara* - Out of fright; *manthara* - slow; *carana* - of \[your\] feet; *viharam* - with the movement; *mukharita* - jingling; *mani* - of jewels; *manjiram* - \[and\] with anklets; *upaihi* - go to \[Krsna\]; *vidhehi* - Make; *marala* - of the swans; *nikaram* - a defeat.

**Перевод:**

**“Go to him now, with your feet moving slowly with fright, with the sound of your jeweled anklets. О Radha, endowed with heavy breasts and hips! Defeat the swans.**

**“O beautiful Radha...**

**Комментарий:**

“O friend! Go to Krsna (*upaihi*).”

In what manner?

“Go while placing your feet slowly, with some fear.” The reason is given, by addressing her with descriptive terms. О Radha, with very heavy breasts and hips! Defeat (*nikaram vidhehi*) the swans by your graceful gait and by the sound of your anklets. It is hinted that this time is suitable for meeting, since now, while the swans are making noise, people will think that the sound of her anklets is the sound of swans. The sound of swans is also auspicious for journeys. Therefore you should not delay in going. It is said in *Vasanta-raja*: *kasthasu sarvasv api darsanena hamsasya sabdena sa sarva-siddhih*: everything said in conversations becomes accomplished by the sound or sight of swans. *Marala* is a swan according to *Dharani. Nikara* means defeat according to *Visva-prakasa*.

### Text 11.4

*srnu ramaniyataram ramani-jana-*  
*mohana-madhuripu-ravam \|*  
*kusuma-sarasana-sasana-vandini*  
*pika-nikare bhaja bhavam \|\|4\|\|*

*mugdhe ! madhu-mathanam...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*srnu* - Hear; *ramamyataram* - attractive; *ramani-jana* - the young women-folk; *mohana* - enchanting; *madhuripu* - of Madhuripu (Krsna); *ravam* - the sound; *kusuma-sarasana* - Of Cupid; *sasana* -the orders; *vandini* - \[as they\] follow; *pika* - of cuckoos; *nikare* - the flocks; *bhaja* - take shelter; *bhavam* - in \[Krsna’s\] love.

**Перевод:**

**“Hear the attractive sound of Krsna’s voice which enchants the young women. As flocks of cuckoos follow the order of Cupid, take shelter of Krsna’s love.**

**“O beautiful Radha...**

**Комментарий:**

The *sakhi* describes other *uddipanas* (stimuli). Hear the sweet speaking (*ravam*) of Krsna, which bewilders young women with its exquisite sweetness. Or, hear the sound of Krsna’s flute glorifying your qualities and which bewilders with its astonishing *svaras, gramas, murchanas, tanas, talas* and *ragas*, speaking sweetly. That sound is most attractive (*ramamyataram*), and thus bewilders the women. Another version has *madhupa-viravam*: the sound produces various *rasas* for the bees. Or, hear the sound of Krsna who tastes *madhurya-rasa* (*madhupa*). And by hinting that he is a bee, it suggests that Krsna is greedy for Radha’s *rasa* and absorbed in singing her qualities.

*madhuraya gira valgu-vakyaya*  
*budha-manojnaya puskareksana*  
*vidhi-karir ima vira muhyatir*  
*adhara-sidhunapyayayasva nah*

**\[The** ***gopis*** **said,\] “O lotus-eyed one, by your sweet voice and charming words, which attract the minds of the intelligent, we have fainted. Our dear hero, please revive your maidservants with the nectar of your lips.” (SB 10.31.8)**

*ka stry anga te kala-paddyata-venu-gita-*  
*sammohitarya-caritdn na calet tri-lokyam*  
*trailokya-saubhagam idam ca nirlksya rupam*  
*yad go-dvija-druma-mrgah pulakdny abibhran*

**\[The** ***gopis*** **said,\] “Dear Krsna, what woman in all the three worlds wouldn’t deviate from religious behavior when bewildered by the sweet, drawn-out melody of your flute? Your beauty makes all three worlds auspicious. Even the cows, birds, trees and deer manifest the ecstatic symptom of bodily hair standing on end when they see your beautiful form.” (SB 10.29.40)**

The flocks of cuckoos are offering respects to Cupid’s order (*kusuma-sarasana-sasana*). They are carrying out his orders. As they do so, take shelter of love (*bhavam*) of Krsna. Or, while the cuckoos sound Cupid’s order, \[you\] make manifest their intentions (*bhavam*). Manifest sounds like the cooing of cuckoos when you experience bliss with Krsna during love. *Vandina* is one who praises according to *Amara-kosa.* The chirping of a flock of cuckoos is an auspicious sign indicating Krsna’s sweet voice, like someone singing and playing instruments while travelling.

### Text 11.5

*anila-tarala-kisalaya-nikarena*  
*karena lata-nikurambam \|*  
*preranam iva karabhoru karoti*  
*gatim prati munca vilambam \|\|5\|\|*

*mugdhe ! madhu-mathanam...*

**Пословный перевод:**

*anila* - In the waving; *tarala* - trembling; *kisalaya* - of shoots; *nikarena* - with the clusters; *karena* - hands; *lata* - of creepers; *nikurambam* - the assemblage; *preranam* - encouragement \[to you\]; *iva* - as if; *karabhoru* - о woman whose thighs \[resemble\] an elephant’s trunk (Radha); *karoti* - makes; *gatim* - going; *prati* - towards; *munca* - Forsake; *vilambam* - procrastination.

**Перевод:**

**“O Radha with graceful thighs! Do not procrastinate in going. All the creepers encourage you to go by waving their bud clusters, like hands, in the wind.**

**“O beautiful Radha...**

**Комментарий:**

О Radha, with thighs curved like the back of the hand! Do not waste time in going. All the creepers encourage you with their clusters of buds quivering in the breeze like gesturing hands. Not able to tolerate your unsuitable behavior, by waving their buds, they urge you on, as if saying, “Do not delay.”

In *Sakuna-sastra* it is said:

*vame madhura-vak paksi vrksah pallavito'gratah \|*  
*anukulo vahan vayuh prayane subha-sucakah \|\|*

**Pleasant breeze, trees with buds on their tips, birds, and sweet talking on the left indicate auspiciousness in going on a trip.**

*Nikurambam* means ‘all’ according to *Amara-kosa*. *Karabha* means trunk of a young elephant. The ornament called preyas is used.

### Text 11.6
